# R&C1 hook points: hook id -> relocation spec (rac-rs, lane omni). A spec is a masked FNV hash of a retail function's code words
# (no code bytes): oracle/src/hs_reloc.rs installs the mod bridge at the entry of every recompiled function (any level, either disc)
# whose code equals it with the relocated fields masked. Written by tools/make_reloc_data.py from hsreloc.py spec (functions with a
# recompiler template) and tools/make_spec.py (the others: the 19 level copies compared word by word, differing words masked). A hook
# id that is not listed here is "not supported on this port".
# USA: the specs match both discs. tools/widen.py (scratch_usa_am, 2026-10-26) adds the fields that the USA build relocates (16-bit immediates of data accesses, j/jal targets) to the masks of every hook
# whose PAL spec was made from the PAL level copies only; the PAL match set of every hook is unchanged (checked: same functions), each hook matches one function per level on USA.
